## Strategies

- `type-balanced`: breadth: the allowance is spread over the job types by weight (agents.scheduler.type-weights); inside each type the highest-priority goals first, oldest as tiebreak, so every goal advances
- `goal-first` (in effect): depth first: the highest-priority goals, oldest first, take the allowance; newer goals of the same priority wait until the older ones have nothing to run (aging and max-wait bound the wait)
## why waiting
# #ID propose-articles zzfocus-g15

- manager: seed:zzfocus-g15
- status: pending
- _capacity_: no place free: 3 run(s) at once (agents.max-concurrent-runs), 3 occupied; goal-first: goal zzfocus-g20 (priority 1000, created DAY) goes before goal zzfocus-g15 (priority 5, created DAY)
- place in the order: 1
- behind: #ID acquisition-sweep zzfocus-g20, #ID currency-check zzfocus-g20
## why running
# #ID currency-check zzfocus-g20

- manager: watch:zzfocus-g20
- status: active (run RUN)
- _running_: its run is queued
## why unknown
no manager item 'zzfocus-nope' (an item id, or the target of an open item)
## scheduler status type-balanced
# Scheduler: type-balanced (set by a person)

- last pass TS
- runs at once: 3 of 6 occupied (agents.max-concurrent-runs; a run waiting for a person holds no place)
- plan pools: chatgpt-plan 0 of 2, claude-plan 0 of 2
- aging: 1 level per 24 h · max wait 72 h · min share 0.0 · gathering places 1 · pool overcommit 2

- #ID propose **zzfocus-g05** (seed:zzfocus-g05; goal zzfocus-g05; priority 0; waited 12 min) — _blocked_: its articles in progress come first (1 to pass review): a new proposal waits until they are published and reviewed

## Strategies

- `type-balanced` (in effect): breadth: the allowance is spread over the job types by weight (agents.scheduler.type-weights); inside each type the highest-priority goals first, oldest as tiebreak, so every goal advances
- `goal-first`: depth first: the highest-priority goals, oldest first, take the allowance; newer goals of the same priority wait until the older ones have nothing to run (aging and max-wait bound the wait)

…and 7 more

## Strategies

- `type-balanced`: breadth: the allowance is spread over the job types by weight (agents.scheduler.type-weights); inside each type the highest-priority goals first, oldest as tiebreak, so every goal advances
- `goal-first` (in effect): depth first: the highest-priority goals, oldest first, take the allowance; newer goals of the same priority wait until the older ones have nothing to run (aging and max-wait bound the wait)

=== goal-plan manual: the plan's run waits for a person and holds no place; the next goal's work takes it

## Strategies

- `type-balanced`: breadth: the allowance is spread over the job types by weight (agents.scheduler.type-weights); inside each type the highest-priority goals first, oldest as tiebreak, so every goal advances
- `goal-first` (in effect): depth first: the highest-priority goals, oldest first, take the allowance; newer goals of the same priority wait until the older ones have nothing to run (aging and max-wait bound the wait)
## drained: autopilot
# Autopilot: ON (since TS, by test)

- **DRAINED since TS (draining since TS by test): nothing runs and nothing new starts — safe to stop the server; `histmersion agent autopilot undrain` lifts it**
- **the managers' crons are off (agents.scheduler.enabled = false, HISTMERSION_AGENTS_SCHEDULER): each watcher sweeps once and never again, and no seeding manager proposes again — new sources are not looked for**
- approvals: goal-plan person (5 a day, sub-goals above depth 2), article-plan person (6 articles per goal a day), article-publish person (10 a day, filter at least 3 sources)
- legal open-access ingests today: 0 of 100 (all watchers)
- 0 legal open-access works in the ingest backlog
- acquire re-check: 0 of 0 items with a DOI due, 0 closed and 0 found this week, 0 of 40 tried today (held while the autopilot drains)
- runs at once: at most 3 (agents.max-concurrent-runs)
- last backup: never by the autopilot · last lint --fix: never by the autopilot
- library: enrich active (idle); review active (1 pending; the scheduler: drain — draining since TS (by test): nothing new starts until it is lifted (histmersion agent autopilot undrain))
